## Inference Research Guidance Check

- `by-structure.md` classifies vtables as `source-declared/generated-binary`: source declarations must exist, but raw table bytes must not be hand-authored. It also requires a minimal formal covered-by comment for reconstructable source-covered items instead of an empty emitter.
- Existing documentation assumptions treated as uncertain were the head-to-next-head physical range convention, `0x0061cf48-0x0061cf53` as metadata/padding, `0x0061cf74` as merely an adjacent caveat inside UID0003OO, the sufficiency of comment-only runtime helper formals, and the non-const runtime descriptor model.
- Direct IDA fact: exact bytes, addresses, names, function bodies, xrefs, RTTI descriptors, read-only PE section, and absent/current comments/types.
- Documentation evidence at Gate 1 pre-callback: UID-linked owner/emitter/source routing, then-current formals and scores, the generated empty marker, manual coverage rows, and same-shape corrected-vtable precedent UID0003PB. The ordinary callback changes are separately recorded by the reconciled ledger, validator results, hashes, and current-state sections.
- Inference: human source identifiers and macro-free explicit source shape. The binary strongly constrains the four-field layout and method behavior but cannot recover exact original spelling.
- Stale Wave2/Wave3 mentions in `by-structure.md` and historical docs were ignored as instructed. No recommendation relies on Wave2/Wave3 output.

## Heuristic / Inference Reanalysis And Validation

- Range issue: exact bytes and RTTI prove `0x0061cf74 -> 0x00649e18`, `??_R4LogoPane@@6B@`, followed by `??_7LogoPane@@6B@` at `0x0061cf78`. The best correction is a UID-preserving endpoint/path change to `0x0061cf74`, not an internal mixed-owner caveat.
- Slot names: `0x004f4b90` is the MSVC scalar deleting destructor generated from `virtual ~LObject()`; `0x004f4b10` is `LObject::GetRuntimeClass`; `0x0041b6c0` is `LObject::OnChangeMessage`. These roles are direct binary/documentation facts. The raw wrapper is not source-authored.
- Runtime descriptor layout: `[0x0061cf44,0x0061cf54)` is exactly four dwords: class-name pointer, object size, optional create callback, and base descriptor pointer. The two zero fields are meaningful null fields, not padding.
- Runtime descriptor names: `RuntimeClass`, `m_className`, `m_objectSize`, `m_createObject`, and `m_baseClass` are high-probability inferred names based on exact field use, existing project naming, and the MFC-like runtime-class architecture. They are preferred over raw offsets and `off_61CF44`.
- Helper names: `RuntimeClass::CreateObject`, `RuntimeClass::IsDerivedFrom`, and `LObject::IsKindOf` are high-probability inferred names. The bodies at `0x005568d0`, `0x005568e0`, and `0x004f4b20` implement those exact roles. The precise original macro/API spelling is not recoverable, but leaving them as comments or raw helper labels is less source-correct.
- Constness: the descriptor resides in PE `.rdata` with section characteristics `0x40000040` (readable, non-writable, non-executable), no writes were found, and all consumers read it. The accepted ordinary source type is `static const RuntimeClass`, with const accessors/helpers; it superseded the Gate 1 pre-callback non-const forward-only source prototype. B004's bounded read-only IDA snapshots recorded the separately detailed I01-I13 prestates and no B004 mutation; any later IDA disposition is authoritative externally.
- Derived-override compatibility: the Gate 1 pre-callback ordinary/generated source search found exactly two `GetRuntimeClass` declarations, in LObject and Pane, while live `entity_query` found only `LObject__GetRuntimeClass` at `0x004f4b10` and every derived-vtable occurrence pointed to that inherited function. The accepted callback removed Pane's redundant declaration; current ordinary-document readback contains the LObject declaration only and no derived definition, declaration, thunk, or override needing a signature edit.
- Full UID0003OP audit: `[0x0061cf74,0x0061cffc)` is exactly `0x88`/136 bytes, SHA256 `53EB10488962DECEC590E14F1249AE1900598B11205FE96F135A8A1CC7F2AC57`, and 34 dwords: three COL pointers, 18 primary slots, 11 EventHandler-view slots, and two TimerHandler-view slots. The RTTI hierarchy enumerates `LogoPane`, `Pane`, `GrafPort`, `LObject`, `EventHandler`, and `TimerHandler`; constructor/destructor/scalar-wrapper stores reach all three table heads.
- UID0003OP source-slot correction: primary `+0x44` (`0x0061cfbc -> 0x004f4f70`) is `LogoPane::OnPaint`; secondary `+0x04` (`0x0061cfc8 -> 0x004f4fe0`) is `LogoPane::HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(Event *)`; secondary `+0x08` (`0x0061cfcc -> 0x004f4fa0`) is `LogoPane::HandleKeyOrTextEvent(Event *)`; tertiary `+0x04` (`0x0061cff8 -> 0x004f5030`) is `LogoPane::OnTimer`. The existing Blit/Advance/OnMouseDown names are useful historical assumptions but are not compile-consistent final virtual names.
- Callback/successor correction: `0x0061cffc` is the decorated `PlainMemberFunctionObject0<LogoPane>` COL pointer; `[0x0061cffc,0x0061d014)` is the exact 24-byte callback COL-plus-five-slot record, SHA256 `96601A3A2006DDEA42550B1D1AD76967F12C36455299EC03CB3B9C1768B1C2A9`; `0x0061d014 -> 0x00649eec` is the decorated LogoPlayerPane COL pointer and belongs to UID0003OR.
- Source placement: declarations belong in `NexusTK/util/LObject.h`; descriptor and helper definitions belong in `NexusTK/util/LObject.cpp`. Vtable bytes and MSVC scalar wrapper remain compiler output.
- Rejected range alternative: retain `[0x0061cf68,0x0061cf78)` as a physical span. Rejected because UID0003PB already demonstrates the current project correction for the identical three-slots-plus-successor-COL error, and because one semantic by-memory page should not own `LogoPane` compiler data.
- Rejected range alternative: extend UID0003OO backward to include `0x0061cf64`. Rejected because UID0003ON already owns `[0x0061cf44,0x0061cf68)` and can accurately document the LObject COL tail without creating overlap.
- Rejected source alternative: hand-author `void *LObjectVtable[]`. Rejected because compiler RTTI/vtable layout must arise from normal virtual declarations.
- Rejected source alternative: keep all runtime-class pages comment-only. Rejected because generated `LObject.cpp` then lacks the accessor and descriptor required to reproduce the live slot and data record.
- Rejected source alternative: preserve `RuntimeClass *GetRuntimeClass()` solely because current IDA has that applied type. Rejected as source-final authority because IDA's `RuntimeClass` is incomplete, the data is read-only, no writes exist, and const qualification does not alter the observed x86 virtual ABI.
- Rejected ownership alternatives: UID00025O is only a mixed physical container; UID0003OP owns the successor LogoPane family; UID0000KV is the file route but not narrower than class UID00007D; UID000373 owns compiler-wrapper evidence but not the table declaration.

## Evidence Standards Used

- Evidence types: dated healthy read-only IDA MCP health snapshots, exact item inspection, bytes, names, function analysis, decompilation/disassembly, comment-channel reads, xrefs, bounded full-image pointer-pattern scans, RTTI graph decoding, PE section characteristics, current by-* docs, generated source, research tracker, manual coverage rows, and matching executed reports. Historical supervisor Gate 2B evidence records two separate failed unsaved I05 typing attempts. First, `make_data` created the intended 16-byte typed item but deleted the real `0x61cf44 -> 0x61cf54` dref; `set_op_type` returned `ok:false`. Second, `set_type({addr:0x0061cf44,kind:global,ty:'const RuntimeClass'})` collapsed the four heads into one item named `stru_61CF44`, removed the class-name dref, emptied `xrefs_to 0x61cf54`, and exposed bogus target `0xff0000000000df32`. The supervisor saved neither transaction and restarted MCP after each. The first 2026-08-04 MCP initialization attempt timed out while the listener and Python process remained live; after a bounded wait, initialization and all required read-only calls succeeded, so this was treated as server-busy evidence rather than an unavailable-MCP exception. B004 performed no IDA mutation/save, coverage/generated edit, generated refresh, execution, or lifecycle command. Eighteen authorized ordinary-document validators used `--no-generated-refresh`; all later command-`20963`/`20971`/`20973`/`20975`/`20976` generated authorities were produced outside B004 and only read back here.
- I05 tool-safety recheck: the dated accepted MCP inventory had no add/create-dref mutator. `make_data` deletes/recreates data, while `set_type` and `type_apply_batch` reach the same `ida_typeinf.apply_tinfo` global-type path proven destructive at this exact address; `infer_types` is also an applying tool and is not a safe substitute. `set_op_type` operates on instruction operands and already failed here. In contrast, data `rename` calls only `idaapi.set_name`; its historical fresh-session `8b3ae691` dry run for `off_61CF44 -> LObject__s_runtimeClass` returned one success with zero failures, and `set_address_repeatable_comments` calls only `idaapi.set_cmt`. The safe disposition therefore performs no I05 global-type application, preserves the offset record/xrefs, and limits I05 mutation to the exact rename/comment pair with immediate readback. Before reliance, the acting supervisor must dynamically confirm current MCP health/capabilities and the complete I05 prestate rather than assume the dated inventory remains current.
- I04 frame recheck: dated live `stack_frame(0x004f4b90)` evidence from healthy session `48fa5591` returns exactly `var_C` `+0x08` `_DWORD`, `var_4` `+0x10` `_DWORD`, `__saved_registers` `+0x14` `_DWORD`, `__return_address` `+0x18` `_UNKNOWN *`, and `arg_0` `+0x1c` `int`. Name remains `sub_4F4B90`; all four function/address comment channels remain absent; instruction comments remain `buffer`, `this`, and `StackCookie`. The accepted MCP `set_type` behavior is known to rename/retype stack arguments when applying the accepted prototype, so I04 must authorize exactly the `+0x1c` name/type delta while protecting every other frame field; a dynamically selected healthy session must reread that prestate before mutation.
- I01 health-order recheck: `b004-gate2b-live` proves the completed I01 UDT itself reads back exactly while the server health readiness flag can remain false without an active-analysis indication. I02 also reads back exactly in that unsaved state. I03's old post-I01 flag gate stopped continuation under the then-current conservative rule; that stop and rollback remain valid history, but the true-only rule is superseded. Because I02, I03, I04, I05 rename/comment, I06, and I07 require only the existing opaque RuntimeClass/LObject declarations or no UDT dependency at all, they can be completed and saved in health-qualified Phase A before I01. Phase C acceptance depends on `status:ok`, correct session/path, `is_analyzing:false`, required capabilities, exact bounded I01-I13 persistence/protection readbacks, and no save mismatch; the readiness flag is contextual and need not return true.
- Xref API/session-state recheck: installed `api_analysis.py` implements `xref_query` by enumerating the current in-memory `idautils.XrefsTo/XrefsFrom` database, filtering by code/data, and deduplicating only the literal `(direction, from, to, type)` tuples already present; its `total` is computed after that dedup and before pagination, with no auto-analysis wait or cache-state normalization. The same file implements `find_bytes` as a direct mapped-byte scan from `inf_get_min_ea()` to `inf_get_max_ea()` through the compatibility byte searcher, advancing one byte at a time. Therefore cross-session aggregate modeled totals are diagnostic session-state observations, while completed full-image raw match sets are byte-state invariants for the same mapped image.

Ledger health policy for C140-C160: `health-qualified`, `healthy`, and `analysis-ready` in these static transaction rows mean `server_health.status:ok`, the correct responsive session and canonical IDB path, `idb_list.is_analyzing:false`, required Hex-Rays/string capabilities, and successful exact bounded IDB-backed prestates/readbacks/protections. They do not require `auto_analysis_ready:true`; either flag value is contextual and false alone is nonblocking. Stop instead for a missing listener/session, wrong path, active analysis/busy state, non-ok health, required capability failure, repeated bounded-query failure/timeout, deterministic prestate/protection drift, or save/persistence mismatch.

- Pane/EventHandler/TimerHandler contracts resolve the four live LogoPane overrides: `OnPaint`, `HandlePointerOrMouseEvent`, `HandleKeyOrTextEvent`, and `OnTimer`. These source identities are compile-consistent and replace the helper-shaped historical names.
- The Gate 1 pre-callback repository-wide ordinary/generated source search found only LObject and Pane accessor declarations, and dated live IDA snapshots found only `LObject__GetRuntimeClass`. All derived table entries inherit `0x004f4b10`; current ordinary-document readback confirms Pane's redundant declaration was removed and no derived declaration remains.
- UID000373, UID0000WM, UID000375, UID00023R, generated LObject output, and same-shape UID0003PB independently corroborate the compiler/source split and endpoint convention.

## Ranked Ownership Analysis

### 1. UID00007D LObject class

- Evidence for: exact decorated vtable/RTTI identity, four-byte LObject runtime size, constructor/destructor/wrapper vptr stores, source virtual declarations, and valid emitter route through UID0000KV.
- Evidence against: none material. The exact vtable bytes are compiler output rather than a handwritten class member array.
- Decision: retain as canonical owner and emitter; use covered-by formal rather than raw table source.

### 2. UID0000KV LObject source file

- Evidence for: descriptor/accessor/helper definitions belong in `NexusTK/util/LObject.cpp`, and the class routes here.
- Evidence against: it is broader than the direct class owner and should not replace class ownership.
- Decision: retain as source root through UID00007D; update source-role documentation, not target owner.

### 3. UID00025O / UID0003OP / UID000373

- Evidence for: UID00025O physically contains the target; UID0003OP owns the successor LogoPane family; UID000373 supplies the first slot compiler target.
- Evidence against: container proximity, successor adjacency, and slot-target evidence do not own the LObject declaration.
- Decision: reject as target owner. Use UID0003OP only for the corrected `0x0061cf74` handoff and UID000373 only as compiler-support evidence.

### Proposed new file/grouping, if applicable

- Proposed owner/name/path: no new source file. Keep `NexusTK/util/LObject.h` and `NexusTK/util/LObject.cpp`.
- Likely full contents: `RuntimeClass` declaration/methods, `LObject` declaration/static runtime descriptor, constructor/destructor, runtime accessor, membership API, change-listener wrappers, and default callback.
- Candidate related items that belong: UID0003ON, UID000371, UID000375, UID00023R, UID00036Z, UID000370, UID0000WM.
- Candidate related items rejected: UID000373 handwritten body, raw vtable/COL arrays, LogoPane successor data, unrelated MemoryMan global operator wrappers.
- Standalone/narrow/broad inference: one coherent LObject utility module; no new runtime-class `.cpp` is justified by current placement.

## Source Placement

- Recommended source placement: type/class declarations in `NexusTK/util/LObject.h`; runtime descriptor and method definitions in `NexusTK/util/LObject.cpp`; compiler-generated vtable/COL and deleting wrapper have no handwritten definitions.
- Placement evidence: UID00007D already routes to UID0000KV, generated LObject source contains all other class children, runtime helper island is already file-owned by UID0000KV, and no competing module owns the runtime descriptor.
- Rejected placements: `LogoPane.cpp` (successor only), `MemoryMan.cpp` (allocator consumer only), a standalone `RuntimeClass.cpp` (no separate source-boundary evidence), and raw `.rdata` tables in any source file.
- Remaining uncertainty: original build may have used declaration/implementation macros. That affects source spelling, not behavior or module placement; explicit definitions are the safest current human reconstruction.

## Range / Split / Padding / Reclassification Analysis

- UID0003OO is exactly `[0x0061cf68,0x0061cf74)`: three LObject slots, no padding.
- UID0003OP is exactly `[0x0061cf74,0x0061cffc)`: three LogoPane COL-plus-table views, no successor callback COL.
- UID0003OQ is exactly `[0x0061cffc,0x0061d014)`: callback COL plus five slots, no LogoPlayerPane COL.
- UID0003OR begins at `0x0061d014` and retains the current `0x0061d0a8` endpoint. This assignment proves its start but does not claim a new full-table score.
- UID0003ON retains `[0x0061cf44,0x0061cf68)` and owns the preceding LObject COL tail cell. UID00025O remains the non-reconstructable mixed parent with corrected non-overlapping children, owner `NONE`, and blank emitter/formal C++ state.
- No corrected UID0003OO byte is padding. UID0003ON's null callback/base dwords are fields. COL pointers and vtable bytes are compiler-generated source consequences, not raw source arrays.
- The exact sequence resolves split/merge ownership without overlap or gap across `0x61cf44-0x61d0a8`.

## Negative Evidence Summary

- No original symbol proves the inferred RuntimeClass field/member spelling. Explicit human names are required so final source does not retain IDA labels; the score cap records this provenance.
- No separate Pane or other derived GetRuntimeClass implementation, thunk, declaration, or unique slot exists. A repository-wide source search and dated live IDA name/function searches both reject a derived override.
- No evidence supports the historical LogoPane helper-shaped names once slots are aligned against Pane/EventHandler/TimerHandler contracts.
- No xref to an individual slot cell is required for compiler vtable storage; exact bytes, table-head stores, RTTI, and inherited pointer reuse establish liveness.
- No source array should be emitted for a vtable, COL, or scalar deleting wrapper.
- UID0003OR does not receive a score increase because only its corrected start was required and rechecked; this bounded disposition is not used to cap UID0003OP or UID0003OQ.
- Original macro authorship remains unrecoverable, but it does not block exact behavior, human-looking explicit source, ownership, ranges, formals, or the recommended sub-95 scores.

- Gate 2B order is three explicit boundaries, each governed by the same flag-neutral health contract. **Phase A, health-qualified analysis-sensitive transaction:** require `server_health.status:ok`, the correct responsive session and canonical IDB path, `idb_list.is_analyzing:false`, required Hex-Rays/string capabilities, and successful bounded preflight of opaque I01 plus all I02-I13 prestates; capture both complete raw pointer sets, six required slot-origin edges, and I08's complete non-truncated 366-site xref result; apply/read back I02, I03, I04, I05, I06, and I07 in that order while I01 remains opaque; re-read I08-I13, both canonical raw sets, all six slot edges, protected frames/comments/items/xrefs, and record aggregate modeled totals, I08 owner-function grouping/unowned-site/capped-sample values, and `auto_analysis_ready` as diagnostics only; save Phase A only when every deterministic I02-I13 result is exact. On Phase A failure, discard the whole unsaved Phase A and verify the original saved prestate. **Phase B, isolated UDT transaction:** cleanly reopen saved Phase A under the same health/session/path/capability requirements plus exact Phase A readback; apply only I01; immediately read ordinal/size/members and all bounded deterministic protections, including I08's complete 366-site count. Either readiness-flag value is nonblocking when the binding checks pass. Save Phase B only then, with no other mutation. On Phase B readback failure, discard unsaved Phase B and reopen saved Phase A. **Phase C, clean read-only acceptance:** close/reopen saved Phase B under the same flag-neutral health contract; read back exact persisted I01 and every saved I02-I13 state, both raw hashes, six slot edges, and I08's complete non-truncated 366-site count without mutation. Stop and report the incident only for a missing/wrong/busy/non-ok session, required capability or repeated bounded-query failure, deterministic drift, I08 count other than 366, or save/persistence mismatch; caller-owner cardinality/sample drift alone is nonbinding. Do not mutate, do not claim Gate 2B complete, and do not erase independently verified saved Phase A/B state. `auto_analysis_ready:false` alone never triggers that stop.
- Global negative constraints: never create functions at raw starts `0x004f4b20`, `0x005568d0`, or `0x005568e0`; never rename decorated `??_7`/`??_R` identities; never create standalone globals for slot cells; never alter bytes/function bounds; never erase ICF/instruction comments.
- Any stop condition cancels save and returns the report to the supervisor with exact current readback; it is not permission to improvise.

## First-Draft C++ Recommendation

- All first-draft/example code below belongs in formal CPP/H headers during the ordinary callback. None is prose-only pseudo-code.
- UID0003OO formal CPP:

```cpp
// The LObject primary-vtable slots at 0x0061cf68-0x0061cf74 are generated
// from the virtual declarations and definitions emitted through UID00007D.
// Do not hand-author a vtable array for this compiler-generated range.
```

- UID0003OO formal H remains blank because the class page owns the declarations.
- UID0003ON formal CPP:

```cpp
const RuntimeClass LObject::s_runtimeClass =
{
    L"LObject",
    sizeof(LObject),
    0,
    0
};
```

- UID000371 formal CPP:

```cpp
const RuntimeClass *LObject::GetRuntimeClass() const
{
    return &s_runtimeClass;
}
```

- UID00023R formal CPP:

```cpp
LObject *RuntimeClass::CreateObject() const
{
    if (m_createObject != 0)
        return m_createObject();

    return 0;
}

bool RuntimeClass::IsDerivedFrom(const RuntimeClass *runtimeClass) const
{
    const RuntimeClass *currentClass = this;
    while (currentClass != 0)
    {
        if (currentClass == runtimeClass)
            return true;

        currentClass = currentClass->m_baseClass;
    }

    return false;
}
```

- UID000375 formal CPP:

```cpp
bool LObject::IsKindOf(const RuntimeClass *runtimeClass) const
{
    return GetRuntimeClass()->IsDerivedFrom(runtimeClass);
}
```

- UID00007D formal H replacement:

```cpp
#ifndef NEXUSTK_UTIL_LOBJECT_H
#define NEXUSTK_UTIL_LOBJECT_H

typedef unsigned int MessageType;

class LObject;
class Message;

struct RuntimeClass
{
    typedef LObject *(*CreateObjectProc)();

    const wchar_t *m_className;
    unsigned int m_objectSize;
    CreateObjectProc m_createObject;
    const RuntimeClass *m_baseClass;

    LObject *CreateObject() const;
    bool IsDerivedFrom(const RuntimeClass *runtimeClass) const;
};

class LObject
{
public:
    static const RuntimeClass s_runtimeClass;

    LObject();
    virtual ~LObject();
    virtual const RuntimeClass *GetRuntimeClass() const;
    virtual void OnChangeMessage(LObject *owner, Message *message);

    bool IsKindOf(const RuntimeClass *runtimeClass) const;
    void RegisterChangeListener(LObject *listener, MessageType messageType, bool allMessages);
    void UnregisterChangeListener(LObject *listener, MessageType messageType, bool allMessages);
    void ForwardMessage(Message *message);
};

#endif
```
